### PR-5 decisions (v2 — each reversible by one word; the fold that produced it in brackets)

- **D-PR5-1 Scope, shape, size and the owner's list.** ONE PR (the owner's "the PR"). **Cut to next-steps:** AC39c, the source's ⋈ *fetch now* (no owner requirement — Q9 names the chat and the scheduler only; it would relax §24.6 item 25 "only under a grant with `refresh: true`", add a grant-less refresh kind and a host word `you` an app can impersonate, and on its main path — the source OPEN — its *stop* cannot stop) [SC2, D12, S13 — "fetch-now"]; AC38's `sqlite_sequence` clause (landed, PR-3). **Estimate** (PR-4's measured ratios: 151 files, +18.2k of which 11.7k tests): (i-a) db — declarations, the marker, the card drops, the rename, `refreshFirst`/`fetched` shapes ≈ 3.0k · (i-b) discovery UI — pairs, the offer, the badge and its line, the act line, the user-ask fixes, the wants note, Settings ≈ 3.8k · (i-c) the history carry (db + loop) ≈ 1.6k · (ii) the chat's cross-app write ≈ 4.2k · (iii) *fetch new data first* + the abort seam ≈ 3.4k · (iv) docs, spec 1.3, whitepaper, threat model, website ≈ 2.5k (generated website pages counted apart) → **≈ 18.5k total**, each partition ≤ 5k. **Gate 5 = five partition reviews (i-a+i-c db, i-b, ii, iii, iv) + TWO security passes with named file lists** — S-a: `access-wants.ts`, `app-provenance.ts`, every import path, `history-carry.ts` + `sync/loop.ts`, `carryOut`'s offer refusal, the pairs' preselection; S-b: `dataTools.ts`'s chat path, `renameDmlTarget`, `access/chatWrite.ts` + `brokeredWriteCore`, `accessWriteCard.ts`'s belts, the import drops, the refresh abort seam. Before Gate 5 the verification lane records `git diff main --shortstat` per partition; a partition over 5k splits again. **The PR body opens with the owner's one-line push ask (after merge) and lists the owner decisions, each with its reversal word:** one PR of ≈ 18.5k ("stack"); Q16 narrowed — the undo journals follow the winner ("journals"); Q15's pairs from declarations only, empty until apps declare ("overlap"); spec 1.3 ("1.2"); the ⋈ on every owned app in a multi-app file ("door"); *don't allow* on a declared ask is a recorded no ("declared-no"); a stop wins across a sync ("stop"); any kit cut ("kit"); the website-deploy window (the sources carry unpushed spec 1.3 text and the final whitepaper); the *fetch now* cut ("fetch-now"); the riders (the schedule-card belt's escape fix, the own `dataWrite` import drop, the two stale closures, the after-run words for a refresh). [SC1, SC6]
- **D-PR5-2 The access declaration — a host-derived mirror of the current HTML, with a SMALL grammar.** `packages/db/src/access-wants.ts` (pure, DOM-free, total — never throws): `ACCESS_WANTS_SCAN_MAX_UNITS = 65_536` (UTF-16 code units, applied by the function itself), `findHeadMeta(html, name) → string | undefined`, `extractAccessWants(html) → AccessWants | undefined`. **Grammar (host-internal; any ambiguity → no declaration, never a refusal of the install):** scan from offset 0 up to the FIRST of `<script`, `<style`, `<body`, `</head` (ASCII-case-insensitive, outside comments) or the unit cap; skip `<!-- … -->`; EXACTLY one `<meta>` whose `name` is `snug:access-wants` (ASCII-case-insensitive), carrying exactly one QUOTED `content` (`"…"` or `'…'`) — two such metas, an unquoted or missing `content` → none; decode ONLY `&quot; &apos; &amp; &lt; &gt;` (any other `&…;` → none; a bare `&` stays literal); the decoded value > `ACCESS_WANTS_MAX_BYTES` UTF-8 → none (no truncation); `JSON.parse` → `accessWantsSchema.safeParse` → `JSON.stringify(value).isWellFormed()` (a lone surrogate from a `\uD800` escape → none) → the value. Browser-vs-host differentials are accepted and stated: the HTML is the app author's own, so whichever parser reads it the declaration is the author's text — only host-to-host determinism matters. **One reader, both paths:** internal `writeAccessWantsFor(sql, appId)` reads `SELECT substr(CAST(v.html AS TEXT), 1, 65536)` of the CURRENT version and writes the row (canonical JSON of the parsed value) or DELETES it; `insertVersion` calls it after its INSERT (every admission: builder commit, revert/reset, starter install/update, shared install/update, the hand-in); plain `run`, never `kvSet`. **Every import, both origins,** deletes EVERY `accessWants:` row first, then writes one per app it holds (a planted or orphan row is never read; a trusted round trip stays byte-identical because the derivation is deterministic — §22.1 rule 5 gains this one derived prefix as a stated carve-out). Accessors on `AccessAccessors`: `getAccessWants(appId)`, `listAccessWants() → ReadonlyMap<string, AccessWants>` (one prefix read). User words never say "declaration" (glossary `:14`): the host says *would like to read*. [S12, F22, SC9 — "grammar" restores the full tokenizer; "keep-trusted"]
- **D-PR5-3 Where a declaration shows.** (a) **The shared install preview only** (no starter carries the meta; the first starter that declares mounts it — a PR-6 plan line) [SC10 — "starter-note"]: `access/AccessWantsNote.tsx` over `extractAccessWants(<the bundle's HTML in memory>)` — ONE sentence `ACCESS_WANTS.preview(app, names, more)` *Budget would like to read data like transactions and accounts from your other apps — nothing is read until you allow it* (`names` = the union of hint tables, falling back to hint words, ≤ 3 then *and N more*; no hint anywhere → *Budget may ask to read your other apps' data — nothing is read until you allow it*), then ONE line `ACCESS_WANTS.forWhat(app)` *what Budget says it's for:* + ONLY the first want's purpose in a bidi-isolated `<q>` [D5]. (b) The Hub tile and Settings (D-PR5-4, D-PR5-7). (c) **The sheet pre-fill:** `startUserAsk(reader, {declared: want, source?})` RE-READS `getAccessWants(reader)` and answers `{kind: 'not-offered', reason: 'gone'}` unless a deep-equal want is still declared [S16a]; it parks a user ask with `askedIn: 'declared'` (pending-only), `hints: want.hints`, the sheet showing the app's purpose, grant provenance `'user'`, and the GRANT's persisted purpose the host's `ACCESS_SHEET.declaredPurpose(reader)` *you started this from what Budget said it would like to read* (app words never sit on a `user` record) [S16c]. The says-line's fourth branch `CONSENT_SHEET.declaredSays(reader)` *what Budget says it's for:* + the quoted purpose (never *would like to: "to …"*) [D4]. **Preselection on the declared and pair forms ticks ONLY tables matched in the TABLE tier** (an exact hint table, or a hint word inside an offerable table's name) — never the every-table fallback; no table-tier match → the source chosen, nothing ticked; never write, `kv`, `refresh`, the away box or a non-default duration [S10]. ***don't allow*** is offered on the declared form only when the normalised hints are non-empty, and RECORDS the want's `accessRequestHash` decline (that want's suggestions disappear; the app's identical runtime ask later answers declined; the act line says so — D-PR5-6a) [S16b, SC16 — "declared-no" makes it session-only]. The reader's plain ⋈ creation act and Settings' create act stay the plain user ask.

- **D-PR5-5 A source offers itself (AC35) — and the ⋈ sheet's two directions.** **The ⋈ widens** (D20's "no empty door" reversed): within today's gate (`!isStarter && allows('access')`) it shows when the app has access state OR the file holds ≥ 1 OTHER app for which `appMayUseAccess` is true; `useHasAccessState` also subscribes to `useLibraryRevision` [F17]; the inverted pins named (`runHeaderIcons.test.tsx:364`, e2e `access.spec.ts:313-314`, `mobile.spec.ts:250` — which must ALSO assert no horizontal scroll at 375 px with schedule, ⋈, share and theme present) [SC11 — "door"]. **The sheet groups each act under its direction heading, rendered whenever the act is** [D6]: under `ACCESS_SHEET.reads(name)` — the reader's best pair act (D-PR5-4) above `access-create`; under `ACCESS_SHEET.readBy(name)` — the offer block, shown ONLY when this app has ≥ 1 registered table and is not a sidecar source and ≥ 1 reader qualifies: `access/AccessOffer.tsx` — heading `ACCESS_SHEET.offerTitle(source)` *let another app read Ledger's data*, `<select aria-label=ACCESS_SHEET.offerPick>` *which app* (`access-offer-pick`; `listApps()` filtered by `appMayUseAccess`, ≠ this app, without a live grant from this app), button `ACCESS_SHEET.offer(reader, source)` *let Budget read Ledger…* (`access-offer`; never `access-create`) → `startUserAsk(reader, {source: appId, offer: true})`. Provenance `'user'`; purpose `ACCESS_SHEET.userPurpose(reader)`. The outcome is recorded under BOTH ids. A later *allow again* of an offered grant is reader-side and may widen to write — named residual. **The user ask's fixes (T1/T2):** a call carrying any of `{declared, source, offer}` dismisses an older pending ask for that reader (nothing recorded — "the user's act wins") and parks anew; on the yield rule it answers `{kind: 'answer-other-first'}` WITHOUT parking; the bare form keeps today's behaviour (pinned). `UserAskAnswer` becomes `'opened' | 'no-app' | {kind: 'not-offered', reason: ExcludedReason | 'gone'} | {kind: 'answer-other-first'}` — the bare form's existing answers byte-compatible for its callers.

- **D-PR5-8 Two-device history (AC37 amended) — both ways; a stop wins on either side.** **History only:** `accessLog:*` lines are carried between the two images in BOTH resolutions; `accessUndo:*` and `ownUndo:*` FOLLOW the winning image (they describe data that went with it — a carried inverse could plant a row the chosen copy never had, and dead entries would hold the 20 live seats) [Q16 narrowed — "journals"]. Identity = the natural key `(at, kind, grantId, readerAppId)` (no `id` seat — a 1.2/1.3-strict older hub would skip and prune such lines first); a match keeps the WINNER's entry with `count`/`rows` = the max; carried entries are those of the loser newer than `lastSyncAt` (all when the sidecar is unknown) whose source app the winner holds; carried `wrote`/`undone` lines lose `undoId` (the winner holds no such entry) [F13, SC3]; merge newest-first by `at` (tie: the winner's first); then the existing prune tiers WITHOUT throwing (carried entries give way first; the winner's protected lines never); the winner's unparseable elements kept verbatim. **A stop wins:** a grant revoked on EITHER side ends revoked — a pulled row whose id is in the local tombstone set, parseable or not, is REPLACED by the local revoked row verbatim (`snapshotLocalAccessGrants` keeps the full revoked row) [S5b]; symmetrically, `pushLocal` applies the origin's revoked rows to the local copy before it pushes [D14]. Renewal mints a new id only after a revoke, so a revoked id is never legitimately re-activated. **Where:** a NEW `packages/db/src/userdb/history-carry.ts` — `snapshotLocalAccessHistory(sql)`, `carryAccessHistory(target, snapshot, {since}) → {carried, dropped}`, `applyTombstones(target, revokedRows) → number`, the prune core lifted from `userdb/access.ts` into a non-throwing helper that `appendAccessLog` keeps using unchanged (its 59 pins). `UserDbImportOptions.carryHistorySince?: string | null` (absent = no carry — the UI's untrusted import and recovery unchanged); `report.history?: {carried, dropped, restopped}` (a NEW seat; `report.access`'s exact pins untouched). `UserDb.carryFromImage(bytes, {since}) → Promise<{carried, restopped}>` (opens a pulled image through the SAME decode `importUserDb` uses; a sealed image it cannot open → `{carried: 0, restopped: 0}`, never a throw — the user's choice is never blocked). **The loop** (`sync/loop.ts`): `pullMerge` passes `carryHistorySince: known.lastSyncAt ?? null` and reads `report?.history` tolerantly; when `carried > 0 || restopped > 0` it anchors `(remote.revision, SYNC_UNANCHORED_HASH)` — a sentinel no sha256 hex can equal — so the next tick pushes [F12, S5a, D14]; `pushLocal` calls `carryFromImage(remote.bytes, {since})` before computing its payload. ADR-0009's status gains "amended by 0076: the access history is carried and a stop wins across a sync; data is never merged". The schedule twin (R-101, an authority revived by a pull) stays open, named [SC12b]. ["one-way", "stop"]

The UNTRUSTED import drops every incoming marker, then writes one for every incoming app whose IDENTITY differs from the local app of the same id — identity = `install_source` + the ordered list of EVERY `(version, pinned, sha256(html))` row (a planted past or pinned version marks the app) [S4]; identical apps keep the local marker state; a fresh device marks every app; a revived deleted app is marked. - **D-PR5-14a The failure words, and two that are not terminal.** ended → *Budget's access to Ledger ended — nothing was changed; allow it again from Budget's access*; not-allowed → *Budget may no longer change Ledger’s transactions — nothing was changed*; busy → *Ledger is in the middle of a change — nothing was changed; try again in a moment*; refused → *Ledger refused this change — nothing was changed*; waiting → *too many of Budget’s changes to Ledger are waiting to be undone — undo some from Ledger’s access, or wait*; gone → *Ledger or its transactions are gone — nothing was changed*; failed → `DATA_WRITE_CARD.failed`. **`busy` and `waiting` keep the acts** (the line sits above *apply*/*cancel* — safe: every apply re-plans with `expectChanges`); `waiting` also offers the access act. [D9]

- **D-PR5-17 The refresh's abort seam, the chip and the after-run words (AC39d).** `access/refreshRuns.ts` (leaf store — imports nothing from `refresh.ts`/`appRun.ts`): ONE entry per refresh spanning its handovers (`{runId, sourceAppId, sourceName, requestedBy, placement: 'live' | 'headless' (updated in place), controller, waiters}`) [F19], `useRefreshRuns()`, `cancelRefresh(runId)`, `abortAllRefreshes('reset')`, `abortRefreshesFor(appId)`. **A door's signal abandons only ITS wait;** the dispatch's own controller is aborted only by `cancelRefresh` (the chip), a reset, or the last waiter leaving [S14]. **A cancelled LIVE dispatch does NOT end its delegated record:** the record is marked `cancelled` — the chip drops it, every waiter gets its answer at once (the frame `ACCESS_REFRESH_FAILED` `retryable: false` with `ACCESS_APP_MESSAGES.refreshStopped`) — while the record KEEPS refusing mutating calls (the refresh posture, never asked), keeps the access door closed and keeps counting until the handler's `schedule-result` arrives or the dispatch's ORIGINAL deadline passes; only then `endDelegatedRun` [S2]. A cancel's line: `refreshed` with `reason: 'failed'`, and `REFRESH_HISTORY_WORDS.failed` reworded *Ledger's fetch didn't finish* (true of a failure and a stop — the PR-3 pin inverted, named; no new token) [D13]; the cooldown stamp KEPT; caches not cleared. **A reset abort (`'reset'`) writes NOTHING** — no line, no fold, no kv clear in the new file — and clears the cooldown map; the engine captures a file epoch at entry and re-checks it after every await (a mismatch returns with no db write); app delete aborts only refreshes whose source or requester is that app [S6]. **The chip:** `RunningChip` reads fetches from `refreshRuns` only (`data-testid="fetching-chip"`); at most ONE fetching chip beside the schedule chip, with *+N* (aria *and 1 more fetch*); its label `RUNNING_CHIP.fetchingIn(source)` *fetching in Ledger* stays visible under 830 px; the act on a HEADLESS fetch *cancel* (aria `RUNNING_CHIP.cancelFetch(source)`), on a LIVE fetch *stop waiting* (aria *stop waiting for Ledger — it may still finish fetching*) [D13]; `runningChip.test.tsx:309` inverted (named). **The after-run words follow the posture:** `DELEGATED_CONFIRM.afterFetchBody(appName, …)` *“Ledger” fetched new data here earlier, so Snug asks every time…* chosen when the touched record's posture was a refresh (a PR-3 copy bug fixed) [D12(ii)]. - **D-PR5-18 The host doors' fetch (AC39b engine) — through the one service.** `access/fetchFirst.ts`: `fetchFirst(db, deps: AppRunDeps, caller: chat | schedule, {signal, run?: {spent(): Calls; deadline: number}}) → {asked: readonly FetchedSource[], calls: Calls}` (`FetchedSource = {sourceAppId, sourceName, outcome: 'fresh' | 'recent' | 'failed' | 'skipped' | 'stopped', at?}`), reached through `await import('../access/fetchFirst.js')` at both call sites [F6]; it lists the caller's admitted grants with `refresh` (a schedule's attendance needs `unattended`), dedupes by source, caps at `REFRESH_FIRST_MAX_SOURCES` (2 — the rest `skipped`), and runs each SEQUENTIALLY through `service.refreshFor(caller, grantId, {deps, signal, run?})` (NEW host-only verb beside the frame's `refresh`, whose wire result is unchanged) — never `refreshSource` directly (P1; a spy pins it) [SC7]. The engine: `refreshSource(db, deps, input, {signal?, fold?: false, spent?}) → {outcome, calls}` (the frame path wrapped, bytes unchanged); `inFlight: Map<sourceAppId, Promise>` consulted BEFORE the cooldown by the host doors (a door JOINS a refresh already running; the frame op unchanged — `refresh.test.ts:715` holds); a joiner races its own signal and deadline without aborting the shared run; busy (one instance) → `failed`. **In-run mode:** the ceiling asks `countersSoFar(db, run.spent() + earlier sources' calls, …)`, `Dispatch.spent` likewise, NO fold, the calls returned. The engine keeps the last settled outcome per source in memory; the words live ONCE in `access/copy.ts` `FETCH_FIRST`: `done(source)` *fetched new data from Ledger first*, `recent(source, when, ok)` *Ledger fetched new data 4 min ago — used that* / *Ledger couldn't fetch new data 4 min ago — used what it had*, `askedRecently(source, when)` *Ledger was asked for new data 4 min ago* (no outcome known), `failed(source)` *Ledger couldn't fetch new data — answered from what it had*, `skipped(source)`, `stopped`; imported by the chat note and the step fact, never respelled [D15b/e]. Words: the editor tick `STEPS.fetchFirst(sources)` *fetch new data first from the apps Budget may read while you’re away* with the hint *now: Ledger — runs Ledger’s own fetch just before, through Ledger’s connections, counted on your daily limits*; offered when the app holds ≥ 1 live PERSISTED grant with `refresh && unattended`; a step already carrying it keeps its tick visible when that fails, with *no app lets Budget ask for new data now — this does nothing until one does* [D11]; `EnableConsent` row *first asks the apps Budget may read while you’re away to fetch new data (now: Ledger)* (sources from a `schedule/fetchFirstFacts.ts` helper every mount site calls) [F8]; `floors.ts` counts a `refreshFirst` step as `REFRESH_FIRST_MAX_SOURCES` × the `app-run` step's cost (an honest "up to") [F10]. ### Acceptance criteria (v2; these replace the Spec section's PR-5 sentences — the originals stay above for history; AC39c moved to next-steps)

- **AC33** The access declaration: `extractAccessWants`' grammar (D-PR5-2: a comment, a meta after `<script`/`<style`, two metas, an unquoted `content`, an unknown entity, a bare `&`, > 2 KiB, past 65,536 units — an astral prefix agrees at admission and after a round trip, a BLOB cell derives nothing, invalid JSON, a schema miss, a `\ud800` purpose, the KB 87 example) → db `access-wants.test.ts`; the row mirrors the current HTML after EVERY admission (install, save, revert, reset, bundle install, bundle update, the hand-in's bundle path) and is deleted when the meta goes; every import (both origins) deletes every row first and re-derives (a planted row and an orphan row lose) → db `access-wants.test.ts`, `userdb.test.ts` (`:1546` green); the shared preview's one sentence and one quoted purpose → `accessWantsNote.test.tsx`; the declared form (re-read, `gone`, the says-line with the KB example — never *to: “to*, table-tier preselection only, *don't allow* only with hints and recorded, the grant's purpose host words) → `accessUserAsk.test.ts`, `accessConsentSheet.test.tsx`; **negatives**: a declaration NEVER writes a grant and never pre-ticks write/kv/refresh/away/duration or an unmatched table; an app's purpose never reaches the Hub tile.
- **AC34** Pairs: `registryTables` + `rankPairs`/`listPairSuggestions` (declarations only; a table-tier match only; every exclusion incl. `share`/imported readers and the session's not-offered set; a hint matching a parsed column ranks in the column tier; an all-credential table never pairs; an app with no schema row is absent; spies on `exportAppRuntime`/`describeAppData` see zero calls; ≤ 5; deterministic) → `accessPairs.test.ts`; Settings' section, rows, *review…*, the act line (allowed + *stop*, wontAskAgain, not-offered words, focus) → `accessSettingsCard.test.tsx`.
- **AC35** The source's offer: the ⋈ widened (+ `useLibraryRevision`; the inverted pins named), the two direction headings, the offer only with registered tables, the reader's pair act, the pinned offer sheet (`offerTitle`, no write toggle, the summary's description never says "change", kv/refresh offered), the outcome on both strips; the user ask's T1/T2 fixes; **negatives**: `carryOut` refuses a forged `write` on an offer and writes nothing; a table-less app shows no offer; `access-create` stays the one reader-side create act → `accessSheet.test.tsx`, `accessConsentSheet.test.tsx`, `accessGrants.test.ts`, `accessUserAsk.test.ts`, `runHeaderIcons.test.tsx`, e2e `access.spec.ts` + `mobile.spec.ts` (375 px, no horizontal scroll).
- **AC36** The tile's access line (visible words in the link's name, both branches, distinct apps both ways, session grants, none at 0, the ≤ 24 h timer, one computation per shelf) and the *could read* sibling (precedence; host words; its aria names both apps; its act line; never *works with*) → `hubTile.test.tsx`, `accessGrants.test.ts`.
- **AC37** The history carry both ways (natural key, max count, `since`, carried `wrote`/`undone` without `undoId`, the absent-source drop, caps never throwing, the winner's unreadable kept, the journals following the winner) and *a stop wins* (a tombstoned id's pulled row — parseable or not — becomes the local revoked row; `pushLocal` applies the origin's revocations; the sentinel anchor so the next tick pushes; a pristine pull with nothing to carry byte-identical; a sealed image it cannot open carries nothing and blocks nothing) → db `history-carry.test.ts`, `sync/__tests__/loop.test.ts` (two-device walks: *use the origin copy* keeps both devices' lines; *keep this device's copy* keeps the origin's lines; B stops → A keeps its copy → after both ticks BOTH hold the grant revoked), `userdb.test.ts`.
- **AC38** The imported marker (identity over every version + pinned + install source; a planted past or pinned version marks; identical apps keep the local state; a revived app; the sweep; trusted keeps; the fail-closed read) → db `app-provenance.test.ts`, `userdb.test.ts`, `delete-app.test.ts`; `'imported'` overrides every install source and its two word forms → `provenance.test.ts`, `accessConsentSheet.test.tsx`.
- **AC39** KB 87's section *Saying what your app would like to read* (the meta right after the viewport meta and BEFORE any `<script>`/`<style>`, its JSON, single-quoted attribute, "declare AND ask"; never a bundle field); every sentence PR-5 falsifies in 87 (`:325-338`), `00-summary.md:12`, SKILL (`:132-146`), `apps/host-mcp/src/instructions.md:42-45`; KB 85's *fetch first* sentence; KB 20's fence unchanged → knowledge `cross-app-access-kb.test.ts` (+ instructions.md and SKILL pins); the KB example parses through `extractAccessWants` → db `access-wants.test.ts`.
- **AC39a** The chat's cross-app write — D-PR5-10…15 → `chatCrossAppWrite.test.ts` (the tool's order incl. the writable-first rule, every refusal by name, `unsafeText` at character 250, the rename's every case, the counting line with no sql, `planChatWrite` never seating, `writeChatApproved` — one case per why row, `grantedAt` after an in-place renewal → `ended`, ok ⇒ undoable), `chatCrossAppCard.test.tsx` (anatomy, acts, the count before apply, no *history* word, the three live undo states, the in-place sheet, busy/waiting keep the acts, the approve guard, the thread swap, the strict rehydrate incl. U+2066), `chatCrossAppTurn.test.tsx` (one card per turn across both keys), `sharedDdl.test.ts`, `materialise.test.ts`, `intentTurnContext.test.ts` (the closing line never cut), `accessProposeTool.test.ts`, `accessCard.test.tsx`, `chatPersistence.test.tsx` (`metaToDataWrite` drops a card with `target` or any unknown key), `writeBroker.test.ts` (the wire bytes unchanged per refusal), db `brokered-write.test.ts` (`renameDmlTarget`), db `userdb.test.ts` (the parsed-key drops incl. JSON-escaped keys; resolved cards stay; trusted keeps), knowledge `access-doors-prompts.test.ts`, host `kitDeadLanes.test.ts` (the swaps, the type mirrors, `chatWrite.ts`'s importers).
- **AC39b** *fetch new data first* — `fetchFirst` (through `service.refreshFor` only; dedupe; ≤ 2 sources; sequential; join in flight; a joiner's stop leaves the shared run going; in-run counting incl. earlier sources; busy → failed; the last-outcome words) → `fetchFirst.test.ts`, `refresh.test.ts`; the scheduler (`refreshFirst`; every proposal channel strips it; ordering before materialise on both branches; the two-clock budget; the AI re-check; a throw keeps the calls; failure never fails the step; the `fetched` seat and `fetchedFact`; `summary` unchanged; the editor's tick, its stale form and its hint; the consent row; the cost) → protocol `schedule.test.ts`, `scheduleExecutors.test.ts`, `proposalWriter.test.ts`, `scheduleEditor.test.tsx`, `enableConsent.test.tsx`, `scheduleFloors.test.ts`, `resultDetail.test.tsx`; the chat (the toggle's conditions and label; consumed only when the door opens; the host note persisted; the context line; *stop* stops the turn; never under webllm/demo/host brains) → `railChatFetch.test.tsx`, `useBuilderChatLanes.test.tsx`.
- **AC39d** The abort seam and the chip (one entry across handovers; a door's stop abandons only its wait; a cancelled LIVE record keeps refusing mutating calls — never asked — until the result or the deadline, then the sticky ask; the headless cancel; *Ledger's fetch didn't finish*; the stamp kept; a reset writes nothing and clears the cooldowns; app delete aborts only its own; one chip + *+N*; *stop waiting* vs *cancel*; the label under 830 px; the after-fetch words) → `refreshRuns.test.ts`, `refresh.test.ts`, `runScopedGate.test.ts`, `runningChip.test.tsx`, `confirmDialog.test.tsx`.
- **AC39e** The final spec 1.3 / whitepaper edition 6 bytes (D-PR5-22) → `check-whitepaper` (edition 6, spec 1.3, the new surfaces, the header-equals-changelog check, `(draft)` refused in `--spec` mode), `check-website-sync`, `schemas-stable` (`git diff main -- packages/protocol/schemas` EMPTY); the threat delta → `check-threat-model`; the push is the owner's act after the merge; Gate 6 greps that no falsified sentence survives outside history and task files (`never offers itself`, `does not change the other app`, `asks for it itself, at runtime`, `read-only here`, `no empty door`, `written and consumed by a later revision`).

### Module contracts (v2 — exact names the lanes share)

- **protocol** `schedule.ts`: `app-think` + `refreshFirst?: true`; the step result + `fetched?`; `REFRESH_FIRST_MAX_SOURCES = 2`; `FETCHED_OUTCOMES`; the proposal-channel strip covers `refreshFirst` (where the authority strip lives); comments updated. No published bytes.
- **db** `access-wants.ts` (`ACCESS_WANTS_SCAN_MAX_UNITS`, `findHeadMeta`, `extractAccessWants`, internal `writeAccessWantsFor`); the `insertVersion` call; `getAccessWants`/`listAccessWants`; the import's delete-all + re-derive; `userdb/app-provenance.ts` (`appProvenanceSchema`, `readAppProvenance(value: unknown)`, `snapshotLocalAppIdentity`, `markImportedApps`); `app-settings-keys.ts` (`appProvenanceKey`, `appIdFromAppProvenanceKey`); `deleteApp` sweep; `userdb/history-carry.ts` (`snapshotLocalAccessHistory`, `carryAccessHistory`, `applyTombstones`, the non-throwing prune core); `UserDbImportOptions.carryHistorySince`; `report.history`; `UserDb.carryFromImage`; `sync/loop.ts` (`SyncableUserDb` widened, `SYNC_UNANCHORED_HASH`, `pullMerge`/`pushLocal`); `brokered-write.ts` `renameDmlTarget`; `dropImportedCards`. All exported through `index.ts`.
- **playground — the shared surface I-1 lands** (types, signatures with throwing bodies, null-rendering components, every mount point, every sentence): `access/copy.ts` (`ACCESS_WANTS`, `HUB_TILE`, `SETTINGS_CARD.pairs*`, `ACCESS_SHEET.reads/readBy/offerTitle/offerPick/offer/declaredPurpose`, `CONSENT_SHEET.offerTitle/declaredSays/userTitle(…, wantsWrite)`, `STRIP_OUTCOME.allowed(…, {notChange})`/`wontAskAgain`, `PROVENANCE.imported`, `CHAT_DOOR.changes/oneChange/valuesIn/readOnlyTable/busy/noRows/unsafeText/plainChange`, `ACCESS_APP_MESSAGES.refreshStopped`, `REFRESH_HISTORY_WORDS.failed` reworded, `FETCH_FIRST`, `CHAT_FETCH`), `schedule/copy*.ts` (`RUNNING_CHIP.fetchingIn/cancelFetch/stopWaiting/moreFetches`, `DELEGATED_CONFIRM.afterFetchBody`, `STEPS.fetchFirst*`, the consent row, `fetchedFact`'s keys), `agent/copy.dataWrite.ts` (`crossLead`, `crossWould`, `crossApply`, `crossApplied`, the whys, the live states); `access/userAsk.ts` types (`UserAskOptions` + `declared?`, `source?`, `offer?`; `UserAskAnswer`; `ChatAsk.access?`) and `startChatAsk`'s three-line `access` body; `access/refreshRuns.ts` whole; `access/refresh.ts`'s new options signature + `service.refreshFor` signature; `access/fetchFirst.ts` signature; `access/chatWrite.ts` signatures; `agent/accessWriteCard.ts` signatures; the components `AccessWantsNote`, `AccessTileBadge`, `AccessActLine`, `AccessOffer`, `AccessWriteCard`, `run/ChatFetchToggle` rendering null, mounted in `RunView` (shared preview, RailChat), `HubView` (the line, the sibling), `AccessSheet` (the offer slot, the pair slot, the headings), `ChatLog` (the card slot); `schedule/fetchFirstFacts.ts` signature.
- **knowledge** KB 87, KB 85, KB 00, SKILL, `data-propose-write.md`, `access-propose.md`; `gen:content` + build. **host** stubs for `agent/accessWriteCard.ts` + `access/AccessWriteCard.tsx`; `vite.config.ts` (+ its "eight modules" header count); `kitDeadLanes.test.ts`.

| Lane | Owns (create or edit) |
|---|---|
| **I-1** (first, sequential) | `packages/protocol/**`; `packages/db/**` EXCEPT `userdb/history-carry.ts` and `sync/loop.ts` (I-1 creates `history-carry.ts` as a no-op stub returning zeros and wires every `userdb.ts` call site); `access/copy.ts`, `schedule/copy*.ts`, `agent/copy.dataWrite.ts`; `access/refreshRuns.ts`; the type/signature/mount surface above incl. its first lines in `access/userAsk.ts`, `access/refresh.ts`, `access/service.ts`, `run/RunView.tsx`, `views/HubView.tsx`, `access/AccessSheet.tsx`, `views/ChatLog.tsx` |
